का . प्रा . 5 04. -- केन्द्रीय सरकार को यह प्रतीत होता है कि इससे उपाबद्ध अनुसूची में उल्लिखित भूमि में कोयला 
अभिप्राप्त किए जाने की संभावना है , 

अतः, अब , केन्द्रीय सरकार, कोयला धारक क्षेत्र ( अर्जन और विकास ) अधिनियम , 1957 ( 1957 का 20 ) (जिसे इसमें 
इसके पश्चात् उक्त अधिनियम कहा गया है ) की धारा 4 की उपधारा ( i ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए , उस क्षेत्र 
में कोयले का पूर्वेक्षण करने के अपने ग्राशय की सूचना देती है , 

इस अधिसूचना के अंतर्गत पाने वाले क्षेत्र के रेखांक सं . एस . ई . सी , एल . जी . एम . ( पी . एल . जी . )/लैंड 142 तारीख 
14 दिसम्बर , 1994 का निरीक्षण माउथ ईस्टर्न कोलफील्ड्स लि . ( राजस्व अनुभाग ) सीपत रोड, बिलासपुर- 495001 के 
कार्यालय में या कलक्टर गोत ( मग प्रदेश ) के कार्यालय में या कोयला नियंत्रक , 1, काउंसिल हाउस स्ट्रीट, कलकत्ता के कार्यालय 
में किया जा सकता है । 

सूचना के अंतर्गत प्राने वाली भूमि में हितबद्ध सभी व्यक्ति उक्त अधिसूचना की धारा 13 की उपधारा ( 7 ) 
में निर्दिष्ट सभी नक्शों , चार्टी और अन्य दस्तावेजों को , इस अधिसूचना के भारत के राजपत्र में प्रकाशन की तारीख से नब्बे दिन 
के भीतर, भार साधक अधिकारी/विभागाध्यक्ष ( राजस्व ) साउथ ईस्टर्न कोलफील्ड्स लि . , सीपत रोड, बिलासपुर को भेजेगे ।

का . प्रा . 505 . -- यत: पैट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का अर्जम ) अधिनियम , 
1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) की धारा 3, उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पंदो 

3, उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पेट्रोलियम एवं प्राकृतिक गैस अधिसूचना का . 
प्रा . सं . 842( अ ) तारीख 24- 11 -94 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट : मियों के उपयोग 
के अधिकार को पाइप लाइनों को बिछाने के लिए अमित करने का अपना भाशय कर दिया था । 

और यसः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 

और प्रागे यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्टिष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 

अब भतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतदद्धारा 
घोषित करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइप लाइन बिछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा अर्जित किया जाता है । 

और प्रागे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत शामितयों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय गैस अथॉरिटी आफ इण्डिया लि . में सभी बाधामों से 
मुक्त रूप में घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है ।

अब अत : उम्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) धारा प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्वारा

के लिए एतदद्वारा अजिस किया जाता है ।

नई दिल्ली , 11 फरवरी, 1995 


का . प्रा . 507. - यतः पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग के अधिकार का पर्जन ) अधिनियम 
1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) को धारा 3 उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन भारत सरकार के पैट्रोलियम एवं प्राकृतिक गैस अधिसूचना 
का . आ . सं . 2567 तारीख 15- 9 -94 द्वारा केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उम अधिसूचना में सलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट भूमियों के उपयोग 
के अधिकार को पाइप लाइनों की बिछाने के लिए अजित करने का अपना ग्रामय कर दिया था । 


और यतः सक्षम प्राधिकारी ने उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) के अधीन सरकार को रिपोर्ट दे दी है । 
- और प्रागे यतः केन्द्रीय सरकार ने उक्त रिपोर्ट पर विचार करने के पश्चात् इस अधिसूचना से संलग्न अनुसूची में विनिर्दिष्ट 
भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार अजित करने का विनिश्चय किया है । 


- अब अतः उक्त अधिनियम की धारा 6 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्ति का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार एतद्द्वारा 
घोषित करती है कि इस अधिसूचना में संलग्न अनुसूची विनिर्दिष्ट उक्त भूमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार पाइप लाइन बिछाने के 
प्रयोजन के लिए एतद्वारा अर्जित किया जाता है । 

और आगे उस धारा की उपधारा ( 4 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्रीय सरकार निर्देश देती है कि उक्त 
भमियों में उपयोग का अधिकार केन्द्रीय सरकार में निहित होने के बजाय गैस अथॉरिटी आफ इण्डिया लि . में सभी बाधाओं में 
मक्स म्प में घोषणा के प्रकाशन की इस तारीख को निहित होगा ।

S. O . 507 - Whereas by notification of the Government of India in the Ministry of Petroleum S . O . 2567 
dated 15 -9 -94 under sub -section (1) of section of the Petroleuni und Minerals Pipelines (Acquisition of Right of 
User in Land ) Act, 1962 (50 of 1962), the CentralGovernaient declared its intention to acquire the right of user in 
the lands specificd in the schedule appended to that notification for purpose of laying pipeline., 

And whereas the Competent Authority has under Sub - section (1) of Section 6 of the said Act , submitted 
to the Government , 

And further whereas the Central Government has, aftor considering the said report, decided to acquirc tbc 
right of user in the lands specified in the schedule aprended to this uotification . 

Now , therefore , in exercise of the power conferred by sub -section (1) of Section 6 of the said Act, the 
Central Government hereby declare that the right of uscr in thc said lands specified in the schedule appended to this 
notification hereby acquired for laying the pipeline ; 

And further in exercise of power conferred by sub -section (4 ) of the section , the Central Governmcat direct 
that the right of user in the said lands shuill instead of vesting in Central Government vests on this date of the pub . 
lication of this declaration in the Gas Authority of India Limited free from cncumbrances .

का . मा . . 508यक्ति केन्द्र सरकार यह अनुभम , करती है कि सार्वजनिक हित में यह है परियोजना के 
पानी पालिस की सप्तमस्मा भी माये, पानी के निकास के लिये परियोजना स्पस से सेंगर नदी तक पाइप लाईन 
बिछाई जाये जो कि गैसः प्रपारिटी माफ : इंडिया लि . द्वारा विछाई जानी चाहिये । 

और यह भी अनुभव करती है कि ऐसी पाइप लाइन बिछाने के लिये इसके साथ संलग्न विवरणी में निर्धारित भूमि 
पर प्रयोगता का अधिकार ग्रहण करना मावश्यक है । 

प्रतः पैट्रोलियम एवं खनिज पाइप लाइन ( भूमि पर प्रयोक्ता का अधिकार ग्रहण मधि नियम, 1962 ) ( 1962 का 
80ख ) के खण्ड 3 के उप खंड ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए केन्द्र सरकार एतद्वारा उस पर प्रयोक्ता का 
मधिकार ग्रहण करने की मंशा की घोषणा करती है : 

बशत कि उक्त भूमि में अपनी चिः रखने वाला कोई भी व्यक्ति मधिसूचना की तारीख से 21 दिन के भीतर 
भूमिगत पाइप लाइन बिछाने के विरोध में अपनी मापत्ति सक्षम प्राधिकारी मंस अथारिटी माफ इंडिया लि . पी . ही . 
पाई . एल . मिलडिंग, ए- 14, सेक्टर-1 भोयग , गाजियाबाद ( उ .प्र . ) में दर्ज करा सकता है । 

और ऐसी मापत्ति वर्ष , कराते समन किसी भी व्यक्ति को यह विशेष रूप से निविष्ट करना होगा कि यह पति 
गत रूप से अथवा विधि व्यवसायक के माध्यम से अपना मत प्रस्तुत करना चाहता है ।

S . 0 . 508 . -- Whereas it appears to the Central Government that it is necessary in the public interest that for 
disposal of waste water from project a pipe line is to be laid by the Gas Authority of India Limited from 
project area to Seogar Rivar. 


And whereas it appears that for the purpose of laying pipeline to supply gas to Petrochemicals Project it is 
necessary to acquire the right of user in the land described in the schedule annexed hereto . 


Now , therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by sub -section (i) of the Section 3 of the Petroleum and 
Minerals Pipeline (Acquisition of Right of User in the Land Act, 1962 (50 of 1962 ), the Central Government hereby 
declares its intention to acquire the right of user therein ;

P . D .I.L . Building , A - 14 , Sector- 1, Noida , Ghaziabad (U .P .).

प्रतीत होता है कि लोकहित में यह आवश्यक है कि मध्य प्रदेश 
राज्य में विजयपुर से दादरी तक प्राकृतिक गैस के परिवहन 
के लिए गैस अथॉरिटी ऑफ इंडिया लिमिटेड , द्वारा पाउपलाइन 
बिछाई जानी चाहिए । 

और यत : यह प्रतीत होता है कि उक्त पाइपलाइन बिछाने 
के प्रयोजन के लिए एतदपाबद्ध अनुसूची में वणित भमि में 
उपयोग का अधिकार अर्जित करना आवश्यक है । 
___ अतः अब पेट्रोलियम और खनिज पाइपलाइन ( भूमि में उपयोग 
के अधिकार का अर्जन ) अधिनियम 1962 ( 1962 का 50 ) , 
की धारा 3 की उपधारा ( 1 ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग 
फरते हए, केन्द्रीय सरकार में उसमें उपयोग का अधिकार 
अजित करने का अपना प्राशय एतद्वारा घोषित किया है ।

के नीचे पाइपलाइन बिछाने के लिए आपत्ति सक्षम प्राधिकारी 
गैस प्रयॉरिटी प्रॉफ इंडिया लिमिटेड , भारतीय विद्यालय चौराहा ,

दम्ब पेंशन मंजर किए जाने के लिए सेना अनुदेश 21म/ 64 से या 
तसवधी नौसेना या वायु मेना अनुदेणों से शामिन होता है और अधि 
नर्षिता की आय प्राप्त करने के पूर्व किसी रेल सेवा या रेल पद पर 
पुननियोजित हो जाता है , इस नियम के अधीन फुटुम्ब रोशन या 
पूर्वोक्त सेना नौमेना/वायु सेना अनुदेश के अधीन पहले से प्राधिकृत 
कुटुम्ब पेंशन की पात्रता के प्रयोजन के लिए निम्नलिखित प्रकार मे 

शासित होगा - - 
(i ) यदि उसकी मृत्य रेल पद धारण करते हुए हो आती है 
सो उसके कुटुम्ब को , इन नियमों के अधीन कुटुम्ब पेंशन या . सैनिक 
सेवा में उमकी मेवानिवृत्ति या उम्मक्ति के समय प्राधिकन फुटम्ध 
मांगन जो भी मुटुम्ब पेंणन, जो भी कुटम्ब के लिए अधिक लाभकारी 
मं हो , की अनुमति होगी । 
(ii ) यदि उसके उसने रेल सेवा में या रेल पत्र पर नियुक्त होने 
पर, पूर्व सैनिक सेवा के लिए सैनिक पेंशन प्रतिधारित करने का

( i ) वह सेवानिवृत्ति होने वाले रेल सेवक को उस महक सेवा को 

अवधि के बारे में प्रमाणपत्र देगा जो पेंशन और उपक्षान और

जानी प्रस्तावित हैं । यदि कार्यालय अध्यक्ष द्वारा यथा उपणित 
प्रमाणित सेवा और परिलब्धियों उसे स्वीकार्य नहीं है जो वह 
अपने बारे के समर्थन म सुसंगत दस्तावेज द्वारा मथित करने

के कारण सुचित करेगा । 
(ii ) सेवा अभिलेखों के अनुपलब्ध होने के कारण प्रहक मेवा की

लिखित विवरण फाइल करने के लिए कहा जाएगा ।

2 . Statement of claim has been fled by the Genoral Sec 
retary , Bank of Baroda Employees Trade Union Congreso . 
Shri Shindo joined the Bank as a Peon in May 1969 and 
came to be promoted to the post of Clerk in July 1980 . 
It is stated that he had a clean service record . Whilo ho was 
working at Ranade Road , Dadar Branch as a Clerk & charge . 
sheet dated 12 - 12 - 1985 lcyolling seven charge wero served 
on him . Charges were serious of Fraud and tampering of 
Banks record was alleged . He came to be suspended and a 
Deperimental Enquiry was instituted by appointing Shri R . L . 
Rao as Enquiry Officer. Ho conducted the enquiry from 
21- 1- 1986 till 4 - 8 - 1986 . Shri Shinde came to be dismissed 
from the services with effect from 27- 6 - 1987. 


he is not as a matter of right, entitled to be represonted by 
an ACiVucute of his choico , It is further stated that as por 
Dipartite Settlement it is at the discretion of the Bank as to 
whether he should be allowed to be represented by an Advo 
cate . Therefore, in my opinion, he cannot complain on that 
ground . Besides , I stated earlier , Shri Gavand , General Sec . 
Tetsry of the Bank of Baroda Employees Trade Union Cong 
Ice had the experience of attending many enquiries and was 
qualilled to defend tlic workman at the cnquiry and has 
done so effectively . Therefore , it is rightly contended that no 
prejudice has been caused as a result. 


3 . The grlovance made is that the enquiry was not fair 
and proper and the same is vitiated on account of non - obser 
vance of principles of natural justice and he has further 
mentioned grounds on which thc order of dismissal is attached . 
Chargeshcot is alleged to be belated . He was not given 
the list of witnesses to be examined at the enquiry nor was 
he provided that list of documents to be produced in support 
of the charges. He was entitled to be represented by an 
Advocate as per Bipartite Settlement and that opportunity 
was not given to him . Ho was not given a fair and proper 
opportunity to defend himself . Witnesses cited namely Shri 
l ednekar and Shri Danait were not examined though they 

enc material witnesses. The Enquiry Officer asked leading 
questions and the findings arrived at hy hlm arc perverse . 
He gave a written explanation in defence which was not 
Considered . 


8 . Non- oxamination of the witnesses cannot be made a 
ground for prievance. It is for the 013nagement to establish 
the charges and cxamine necessary witnesses to prove them . 
Examination of those witnesses was not found necessary and 
therefore , not examined . I therefore , find that there is no 
merit in any of the contentions raised by the union in its 
sta !cmcut of clini and which contentions are not supported 
by the proceedings of the cnquiry which are before me. The 
enquiry is , therefore , not vitiated . The contention is that tho 
findings are pervcrre . I do not acc how it could be urged 
that they are perverse . In fact, the roport dated 28th January 
1987 shows that the Enquiry Officer considered tho material 
that vias placed before him and after examining the same in the 
light of the defence raised he concluded that the charges were 
proved . I am unable to find out Any perversity in the 
findings. 


4. Written statement hag boon filed on bchalf of the man 
agement by Regional Manager , Bombay City Region -III. It is 
admitted that he was appointed initially AN A Peon and later 
promoted as a Clerk and W88 at the releviini lime working 
at Ranade Road Branch , Dadar with effect froin July 1980 . It 
is further stated that he was involved in a fraud case amount 
ing to Rs. 88 ,150 / He had made wrong posting of entrios 
unsupported by vouchers in the account of one of his friends 
and subsequently having withdrawn the wuid aniount from 
the account with the connivance of his friends . As a result 
lie was fuisponded , a criminal prosecution launched against 
him in a Special Court at Bombay . The misconduct was en 
quired into and at the end of the enquiry in which hc 
was given fair opportunity to defend himself and which co 
quiry was conducted in accordance with the principles of 
natural justice the order of dismissal was passed . 


9 . One ground urged is that the chargesheel was delayed . 
There is no delay ay such in giving the chargesheet. The 
prosecution was started and investigation was goin : on the 
pormission of the authorities before the cnquiry was obtained , 
No prejudice is also shown to have been caused as a result 
of that. So far as the punisfiment is concerned , I find that 
The charme was grave enough to warrant this penalty . He was 
on cmployee of the Bank and committed fraud and fabricated 
acronnts and bencfited himself. That being so the penalty 
inficted is thoroughly deserved. 


Award is tlierefore , accordingly passed . 

R . G . SINDHAKAR , Presiding Omcer 


if fatetit , 27 09179 , 1995 


5 . It is denied that he was not given liet of witnesses or 
documents, that he was not given an opportunity to defend 
himself. It is also denied that tho findings are perverse . Tho 
management therefore, submitted that the order of dismissal 
is proper , legal and justified . 


6 . The management WBA reprosented by Shri D Souza and 
ho was heard on the preliminary point about the fairness of 
the enquiry . Thero was no appearance on behalf of the union , 
in spite of the fact that the matter was adjourned on several 
occasions because of the absence of representative of the 
union . Thus no submissions have been made on behalf of 
tho imion .

18 . Third decision in the case between Rambhwal Thakar 
Pragad ano! Phoenix Mills reported in 1976 I LLJ Page 93 
is a case where the worker had gone on leave and on cxpiry 
of leave did not resumo duty but on seeking extension of 
leave on medical certificato management refused to grant leave 
and treated him as having abandoned service , Labour Court 
reinstated but Industrial Court reversed that order the High 
Court held that the cmployee is entitled to offer explanation 
for absence and prove it by evidence . He has to be given an 
opportunily of being heard before legal fiction of abandon 
ment of service is brought in play. This is precisely what the 
employer has done in the case before me. I do not see 
how support can be taken from this decision by the union . 
Tho decisions in the case of Nalinkant J. Baxi, Abpiedabad 
V $. Chairman and Managing Director , Bank of India , Bombay 
and others reported in 1986 Labour Industrial Casco 1784 is , 
with respect not relevant in this case before mo.

19 . In the case betwcen D . K , Yadav Vs . J . M . A . Industries 
Ltd , reported in 1993 II CLR Page 116 , the Supreme Court 
found that the cinployce who had overstayed leave was ter 
minated without notice under Standing Order , an opportunity 
of hearing was not yiven to him . It was held that the prio 
ciples of natural justice must be reud and the impugned action 
was violative on the principles of natural justice . Here in 
this case before me, the opportunity of hearing has been 
given to the employee , she was called upon to resumc duties 
informed that it would be presumed that she had abandoned 
service and in the teeth of, all these the employee remained 
silegt .

20 . Dacision in the case of Ashok Kumar Majumdar Vs. 
United Bank of India & Ors , roported in 1992 II CLR Page 
999 of Calcutta High Court is in respect of the employee 
whose sciv . ces were terminated by the Bank for unauthorised 
absence for several days by serving only show cause notice 
by invoking para 522 ( 1 ) of Shastry Award . There waS DO 
chargesheet and yet the major punishment of termination of 
sorvices was indicted . It was held that it cannot be dono 
and the punishment was set aside , Clause 522 of Shastry 
Award dealt with procedure for termination of employment. 
In this case before me, Clauso XVI of the 1964 Settlement 
is invokod and has been fully and duly complied with , Ashok 
Kumar s case , with respect is a case in which without giving 
a chargeshect, punishment of termination was inflicted when 
absence without leave or over - staying sanctioned leave with 
out sufficient ground as a case of minor misconduct under 
Clauso 1907 ) . It is because of this that the order came to be 
kot aside . I therefore, find that the principle , laid down by 
these authorities have been followed and the management 
bas given the employee an opportunity to ask for extenzion if 
on medical grounds it was necessary and thereafter when the 
directions of the doctor and not complied with and she did 
por resume duties again called upon her to resumo duties 
which she failed to do . She did not go and satisfy the man 
agoment about the reasons for not resuming duties in tho 
circumstances the managemont fell back upon Clauso XVI of 
tho Settlement. Mr. D Souza relied upon a decision in the 
case between T . Venkateswarlu and Branch Manager , State 
Bank of India , Vijaywada , and another reported in 1990 II 
LLN 39 page wherein an employee of State Bank of India sent 
on deputation not rejoining duty in Bank after expiry of 
deputation was treated as having voluntarily retired in terms 
of para XVI of tho Bipartite Settlement contended that it was 
A case of retrenchment. It was held , conditions necessary to 
deem a " voluntary resignation " were satisfied . It was held 
to be not a case of retrencbment. However , I find that in 
this particular case the management has acted fairly in accor 
dance with the principles of natural justice and tho ordot 
passed cannot be faulted on any of the grounds urged ,

not be attracted because it applied to a case of dismissal

of bringing them down by ono giado and excluding the

circumstances of the case there was no justification for tak 
ing depari mental proceeding against tho employees and im 
posing penalties on them . They are set aside.

7 . Since there was no appearance on behalf of the railway 
ndministration oral evidence has gone unchallenged . It ap 
rears , therfeore , that his contention that thero was a tormi 
nation of service is established by the evidence on record . 
It is also evident that the theory put forth by the Railway 
Administration before the conciliation officer that he admi 

isration wanted him to be continuod but he absconden is 
not established on record . 


Bombay, dafod 30th day of December, 1994 


AWARD 


8 . It is the case of the workman and in support there is 
evidence that the termination was not preceded by any notice 
nor followod by payment of retrenchment compensation . It 
in needless to sily that he had since 1984 till 13th cf January , 
1987 workert continuously and therefore his case ought to 
have becn dealt with by following the provisoins of Section 
25F of the I. D . Act . That has not been done as its evident 
from the evidence on record . If the Management fails to 
follow the conditions precedent to retrenchment of workmen 
then the consequence is that the order of termination is bad 
and is liable to be set aside. Further consequence is that 
he continued in service . 


Govergmont of India Ministry of Labour has by lotter dt. 
21- 1 - 1994 made a reference under section 10 ( 1 )(d ) of the 
Industrial Disputos Act, 1947 for adjudication of the dispute 
mentioned in the schedule : 


SCHEDULE 


" Whether the action of the management of Ceutral 

Rallway , Bhusaval in orally terminating the services 
of Shri Namonarayan Pyrolal Pande w .. . f. 14 - 1 -1987 
la proper legal and justified ? If not, what relief 
the workman is cntliled to ? " 


2 . Statement of claim has been filed by the workmen . 
According to him he was engaged as a casual labour at 
Badnera on or about 30th of August, 1984 . He was con 
verted into monthly rated casual labour on or about 19th of 
September , 1985. He contends that he acquired a temporary 
status as he had worked for inoro than 240 days in a year . 


9 . Looked from another point of view also I find ihe 
action of the railway administration is not justified . He 
acquired tonirorary status under the Indian Railway establish 
ment manual, If he acquires a temporary status then termi 
nation is to be dono in accordance with that . The proceduro 
prescribed is not required to be followed if he happens to 
be a casual labour then no notice is necessary but once he 
acquired a toniporary status notice under clause 302 of tho 
manual is necessary and which is not given . It is not shown 
that the case is covered by the provisions of (1) and (ii ) in 
that clausc . In the circumstances I find that the action of 
the management is unjustified , neither proper nos legal. As it 
result he is entitled to reinstatement with continuity of 
service. 


3 . On or about 13th of January , 1987 his services, so con 
tends the workman , wero terminated by oral order without 
issuing either prior notice or without paying retrenchment 
compengation . No opportunity of any hearing was given 
before terminating his services . Repeated qucstions for re 
inktatement Chd no effect. Ultimately by notice dated 28th 
of September , 1990 be caled upon the management to a 
Instate him in service . This also did not get favourable 
response and therefore ho approached conciliation officer 
namely , Labour Commissioner . His efforts also failed and 
consequently the Central Government has made the above 
referred reference for adjudication of the dispute . 


10 . Tho question that then arises is allout hack wages. The 
Learned Counsel appearing on behalf of the workmen natu 
Tally submitted that the workman should be paid back wages 
with reinstatement. I find from the evidence on record that 
during the intervening period this work man 1199 worked 
though with a private company and on his own admission 
earned Rs. 20 per day of course as and when work was 
Available. He further stated that if there was no work he 
was idle . Considering the facts and circumstances of the 
case I would direct the rallway administration 3 / 4th of the 
Wages which he would otherwise , have earned but for this 
order of disinissal since the date of termination which is 
141h of Januaiy, 1987. 


4 . Notice of this was issued to the Management and on 
13th of June, 1994 the Management deputod one Mr. Jagtap 
a clerk to represent the Railway Administration . His request 
for time for Aling written statement was granted , Matter came 
to be adjourned to 16th of August, 1994 and thereafter to 
9th of Noyemher 1994 . Finally it has come up hefore me 
304 . G1/95 - 11 


Award accordingly . 


R . G , SINDHAKAR , Presiding Officer

Before framing the charges the workman was given a show 
causc notice regarding the charges to which he has filed ex 
planation . The explanation of the workman was not found 
satisfactory by the management and the management ordered 
a domestic enquiry . The then cbiet manager Sri. A . S . Romu 
kristinan was appointed as the onquiry officer . He las con 
ducted il comestic enquiry and filed the report finding the 
workmen guilty of the charges , 


2 . In the claim statement filed by tho workman the con 
hentions advanced are briefly as under : The workman joined 
the service of management Bank on 24th June, 1977 and he 
had more than 7 ycars service as clerk till the date of dis 
Dissal. He was compelled to work outside tho State of 
Kerala throughout his period of service except for a period 
of 6 months in Kerala . Because of that he had become gick 
and had to avail leave on medical ground in the years 1989, 
1990 and 1991 during different spells . His applications for 
leave had been duly admitted and sanction was accorded 
till 30th April, 1990 . He had submitted Medical Certificate 
also , while so the management as per memo dated 24th 
November , 1990 framed the charges in question against hini . 
Without properly considering his cxplanation the management 
ordered a domestic enquiry . The enquiry was not properly 
donducted and he was not given sufficient opportunity 10 
prove his innocence. The enquiry was conducted without 
complying with the rules of natural justice and fairness . After 
the finding of Enquiry Officer he was given a personal hear 
ing. But without considering his submissions the management 
or loreal the preacnt punishment. His appeal was also dismlos 
led without proper consideration . The Enquiry Officer directed 
the workman to furnish the list of defence witnesses and 
documents on or before 8th March , 1991 which is illegal and 
wholation of the Tuhes of natural justice causing serious pre 
Didice to him . The Enquiry Officer has furnished the list of 
witnesses and schedule of documents to the workman which 
shows that he was functioning as presenting officer which 
antount to judge prosecutor combination . He was biased and 
much prejudice was caused to the workman . The documents 
submitted by the workman were not accepted by the enquiry 
officer and thereby denied reasonable opportunity to defend 
his case The enquiry was closed abruptly without giving 
opportunity to the workman to lead evidence . The onquiry 
bfficer did not obtain the signature of concerned witnesses 
Or Mat of the workman . The enquiry officer was not correctly 
recording the oral evidence of witnesses causing serious pre 
iudice to the workman . The cnquiry ofñcer has entorod his 
finding without considering the evidence and documents objec . 
tively . Such findings are perverso and inacceptable . While 
saping the dismissal order the manngement has taken into 
consideration the anticident conduct of the workman without 
prior notice to him . Such action is in gross violation of 
principles of natural justice. The charges against the workman 
- 810 vague and no material was also let in to substantiate such 
charges. The enquiry officer fniled to note that the workman 
was unable to report for duty at Hyderabad only because 
of his illness . The punishment imposed is grossly dispropor 
tionale . He is the only bread winner of the family with wife , 
one child and aged parents . He is remaining unemployed 
qi0cc , the date of dismissal and he has no other means of 
hyolihood , Further case is that his dismissal from service is

does not include ( hh ) termination of service of workman as

man concerned . 


11 . Having heard the representatives of the parties , having 
gone through the evidence on record and after considering 
the matter clęcply , to my mind , the reference petition deserves 
to he declinca . 


Thus it would be seen that a combined reading of provi 
sions mentioned above would go to show that retrenched 
persons are those persons , who put in 240 days of continuous 
service . Admittedly , as mentioned above, none of the work 
men llave completed more than 89 days, so , to my mind , 
they can 1700 possibly be termed as retrenched so as to attract 
the benefits provided U / S 25 - F , G & H of the Act.

are that the work for which they were employed was of a 
permanent nature . The services of workmen had been ter 
mmated by the management in violation of the provision of 
the Act. While on the other hand, the case set by the Mana 
ver is that the workmen were employed as temporary clerk 
cum - Tynist, purely on temporary basis , for a specified period 
of 89 Jays . None of the workmen had completed more than 
10 so they have no industrial rights . Ex. W1 is the affidavit 
Paramjit Kaur Bedi in which she was stated that she had 
worked fruni 17 - 6 - 77 10 17 - 9 - 77 As clerk - cum -typist in Sec , 3.5 , 
Chandigarh branch of State Bank of Patiala . Similarly Ex.

from 10- 8 - 78 to 9 - 10 -78 und 17 - 10 - 1978 to 13 - 11 -1978 .

rerial of 89 days. Similarly affidavit of B . R . Singla Ex. M2.

1.1. Now , the short and significant questions , though inpor 
tant, arise for determination in these cases are , who workmen 
are entitled to any relief, ay contemplated under chapter V - A 
of the Act . Section 25 - F of the Act postulated that no 
workinan cinployea in any industry , who has been in contin 
LOUS service for not less than one year under an employer , 
shall be rctrenched by that employer unless (a ) the workman 
has been given one month s notice in writing, indicating the 
reasons for retienchment and the period of notico has expired . 
or the worl:man has been paid in licu of such notice, wages 
for the neriod of the notice , (b ) the workman has been paid , 
at the time of retrenchment, compensation , which shall be 
equivalent to fifteen days average pay for every completed 
year of continuous service any part thereof in excess of six 
months, and (c ) notice in the prescribed manner is served 
on the appropriate Government or such authority as may he 
"neciled by one appropriate Government hy notification in the 
official gazettee . 


The sole contention of the representative of the workmen 
that since the workmen were appointed by the management 
for specified period and their services were terminated without 
complying with the mandatory provisions of the Act, so they 
are entitled for 1c - instatement, is neither tenabale nor the 
judgements relied upon by them in Hindustan Steel Lid . Vs. 
State of Orissa & Others 1976 (33 ) FLR 257, Kurukshetra 
Co -op Bank Llal, and State of Haryana & Others in CWP 
No . 112 (of 1989, Rajbir Singh & Others Vs. State of Haryana , 
1983 ( 1 ) S . L . R . 38 the Municipal Committee Gobindgarh Vs. 
The Presiding Officer , Labour Court, Patiala 1993 (3 ) S . L . R . 
265 are applicable to the facts of the present cases . The 
Crux of the proposition of law laid down in Hindustan Steel 
Ltd . Vs. State of Orissa & Others, Kurukshetra Co -op Bank 
Ltd . and State of Haryana & Others and Rajbir Singh and 
Others Vs, State of Ilaryana case ( supra ) is that , non - comp 
liance of Section 25- F would entitle the employee to be re 
instated in service with full backwages and in those cases , 
the workmen had already completed more than 240 days of 
continuous service . with lotional breaks, which was held to 
be unfair labour practice . Possibly , no one can dispute about 
the proposition of law laid down in the aforesaid judgements . 
The samc would not come to the rescue of the workmen , 
45 they had not completed more than 89 days of service in 
the present case and their appointments were for a specified 
ncriod. In the above mentioned judgements, it had not been 
(lisputed that the retrenchees had already completed 240 days 
of their service . It is now well settled that, if a workman 
Tras not put in 240 days of service , they have no industrial 
rights under the Act, and can not, therefore , avail of the 
machinery under the Act , for the settlement of their dispute . 
The policy of the Act, draws a distinction between those . 
with service of 240 days and more, and other with less . It 
was not necessary for the manavement, in the present case , 
to comply with Nic provisions of the Act, before dispensing 
with the services of thic Ortitioners , as claimed by thim 
Hon ble Supreme Court of India has calegorically observed 
ir naru 1 .39 of a judgement in case of Gujarat Steel Tubes 
I td Etc . Vs. Gujarat Steel Mazdoor Sabha and Others AIR 
1980 S . C . page 1896 , ihat policy of the Act draws i distinc 
tion betiveen those with service of 240 days and more and 
olhers with legs. The workinen with a record of 240 days , 
On the roll, are a class . who have only rights under the Indus 
trial nu?, Reliance in this regard can also be place , ton

So far as the aforesaid first contention challenging the 
maintainability is concerned , the management has led no 
evidence. Even at the stage of argument no evidence was 
pressed into service in support of that plea in the written 
statcment. It may be mentioned that at the stage of hear 
ling argument the management did not press that point 
at all while challenging the majntainability of the reference 
and the proceeding. Nontheless when such a contention 
is there in the written statement it may be mentioned in 
brief that when there has been an admission by the manage 
ment regarding ithe engagement of the workman as a casuall 
temporary worker and when the management nas admitted 
about the category of the second party as a workman and 
also about the termination of his service / refusal of further 
engagement, , thus, it is a case relating to an industrial 
dispute as defined in Section 2 (k ) of the Act. In that 
connection , it is acedless to mention , being the admitted 
position , that there existed relation of employer and 
employee betwecn the management and the workman , Under 
such circumstanco, even on mcrit also the aforesaid con 
tention of the management regarding maintainability is 
non -sustainable 

Coming to the other contention cballonging tivo maintain 
ability , the managemtnt has contended that the mining 
operation never started . M . W . No. 1 in his evidence has 
stated that the mining operation of Bauzito Procot never 
functioned and it was closed because of tho agitation by 
the local people and because clearance certificate from the 
Environmental Protection Department of the Central 
Government could nat be obtained . The workman has 
also in his evidence, admitted that aspoot of the caso of 
the management regarding closuro of the mining operation . 
But the evidenct of M . W . No . 1 . and W . W . No. 1 goes to 
show that such stoppage of mining operation and close 
down of the Project was after 1985 i.e . after termination 
of services of the workman , Therefore , a situation which 
arose after the cause in favour of the workman accrued , 
could not have been taken into consideration by for seeing 
the future and therefore , the Central Government can not 
be blamed for making the reference because by the time 
the reference was made there was no cloguro of the cstablish 
Anont. Under such circumstance, the contention of the 
management challenging the maintainability of the referenco 
is not acceptable .

5 . Though a specific issue relating to maintainability of 
the reference ly , not framed , but both the parties were con 
scious of their pleadings and accordingly they went on 
trial, hence the question of maintainability shall be consi 
dered on the basis of the picadings and tho evidenco as 
available in the record . 


8 . Only at the stage of argument the management bag 
advanced a case that the Central Government was not 
competent to make the reference and therefore , the reference 
is bad in law . In that conneotion it has also relied upon 
tho decision of the Hon ble High Court in O . J . C . No . 5193 
of 1991 (Gandhamardan Baxuite Mazdoor Sangha Vrs. 
Ms. BALCO & others ) wherein their Lordships have been 
pleased to propound that the State Government is the oom 
petent authority to make the referenco . In this connection , 
it may be stated that till the time of Aling of the written 
argument the management never raised such point 
challenging the maintainability of the reference and the 
workman was never confronted with that objection regard 
jag maintainability of the reference . Therefore , such 
belated plea taken only at the stage of filing the writen 
argument is not acceptable . Though the plea was available 
to the management at the time of filing tho writien state 
mont but it has not taken such a plea . Under such circum 
stance , as per the ratio propounded by the Hon ble Supreme 
Court in the decision reported in A . T. R . 1979 S . C . Page 
1652 ( Shankar Chakravarti Vrs. Britannia Biscuit Co . Ltd . 
& another ), a fact Introduced in a case beyond the picad 
inge showed not be considered at all . Pollowing that 
principle of the Hon blo Supreme Court, it can be safely 
said that this helated plea of the management is not enter 
tainable . Hence , the aforesaid two grounds raised , challong 
ing maintainability of the reference is not acceptable at 
this stage and accordingly, the contention of the manage 
ment is rejocted .

12 . Besides the aforesaid factual findings if attention should

13 . The management has categorically stated that the work 
man was given engagement upto 8 - 12 - 85 . It appears from 
Ext . D that experience certificate was granted to the workman 
from 7th February , 1984 to 8th December, 1985. 
Nonetheless from the very beginning i. e. from 
the stage of raising thc Industrial dispute the workman 
hun advanced tho contention that he was refunod employ 
ment from 16 - 12 - 85. In that connection . at the stage of 
conciliation the management did not dispute about the 
aforesaid date of termination which is evident from Ext. 
C /3 . For that reason the reference mentions 16 - 12-85 to 
bo the date of termination of the workman. In his evidence 
the workman has said that though he worked till 16 - 12 -85 
but he was not given appointment from 9 - 12- 85 to 16 - 12 - 85 . 
That cvidence of the workman has not been proved to be 
untrue though the workman was extensively cross - examined 
hy the management. The aforesaid evidence of the workman 
also found credible and also accepted. Thus, the date of 
termination of the workman is 16 - 12 -85 and not 8 - 12 - 85 . 


14 . It is the case of the management that during the 
tenure of his service tho workman never mloconducted ana 
thorofore , there was no disciplinary proceeding against him 
preceding the termination / refusal of employment . It is the 
admitted case of both the parties th: 17 naither il rotrenchment 
notico was sorved nor the workman was paid retrenchment 
compensation . Looking to the facts involved in the case 
and the provision of law in Section 2 ( 00 ) read with goction 
25- F of the Act, it leaves no room for doubt that so far 
as the refusal of employment to the workman is concerned , 
it is a case of retrenchment and in that connection , the man 
agement has not followed the mandatory provisions of law 
relating to a case of retrenchment, in as much as, neither 
retronchmem notice was given mor retrenchment compensation 
was paid in accordance with law . Hence , the termiantion 
of service of the workman with effect from 16 - 12 - 85 is both 
illegal and unjustified .

2 . The second party -workman while working 
as a Dumper Driver under the first party manage 
ment was charge sheeted on 18 - 11 - 85 on the alle 
gation of dishonesty, causing loss to the Society 
and misconduct coupled with indiscipline and dis 
obedience of lawful orders on 9- 9 - 85, 12 - 10 -85 
and 14 - 10 - 85. Accordingly, he was charge sheeted 
and a domestic enquiry was conducted . The en 
quiry officer found all the charges established 
against the delinquent and the Board of Directors 
of the management accepted that report and issu 
ed a show -cause notice and thereafter on considera 
tion of the matter on merit passed the order of 
dismissal against the workman . The workman bc 
ing not satisfied with the aforesaid order of the 
management, raised the present dispute . The Asst . 
Labour Coinmissioner (Central) , Rourkela took 
up the matter for conciliation and when there was 
no settlement, he submitted the failure report and 
basing upon that the above quotted reference was 
forwarded for adjudication . 


BETWEEN : 
The management of Sundergarh Mining La 

bour Contract Co-operative Society Ltd ., 
Contractor at Purunapani Limestone & 
Dolomite Quarry of Mis . Steel Autho 
rity of India Ltd ., P .O . Purunapani, 
Dist : Sundergarh . 

. . First party 
management. 


( And ) 


Their workman Sri Kiran Shoy , represented 

through North Orissa Workers Union , 
P . O . Rourkela - 12 , Dist : Sundergarh . 

. . Second party 

workman . 


3 . In his claim statement the workman has 
pleaded his innocence to the charges levelled . Be 
sides that he has contended that the domestic en 
quiry was conducted without affording proper op 
portunity to the workman to defend his case and 
apart from that the observations of the enquiry 
officer relating to the gravity of the charges and the 
proposed punishment were not at all properly con 
sidered and the workman was not heard before im 
posing the punishment. Thus the workman has 
challenged the order of termination as illegal and 
arbitrary and has prayed for reinstatement in 
service with full back wages .

Sri $. K . Pandey . Secretary - For the first party 

management. 
Sri B , S . Pati, General 
Secretary of the Union . 

- - For the second party 

workman . 


AWARD 


The Government of India in the Ministry of 
Labour in exercise of powers conferred upon them 
by clause ( d ) of sub- section (1 ) and sub -section 
(2A ) of Section 10 of the Industrial Disputes Act , 
1947 ( 14 of 1947) ( for short the Act ) have re 
ferred the following dispute for adjudication vide 
their order No. L - 29012 / 3288 - D . III ( B ) dated 
8 - 9 - 1988 : 


4 . In its written statement the management has 
reiterated its contention regarding the alleged mis 
conduct covered by the charge sheet and has c011 
tended that the workman was given full opportu 
nity to defend his case . According to the manage 
ment, since the proved misconducts were grievous 
and serious, therefore , the Board of Directors were 
free to take the decision relating to termination of 
services of the second party -workman even if the 
cnquiry officer had suggested otherwise . In a nut 
shell , the inanagement has advanced the plead 
ings supporting its order in terminating the servi 
ces of the workman . 

5 . On the basis of the aforesaid pleadings, the 
following issues were framed : 


ISSUES 


" Whether the action of the management of 

Sundergarh Mining Labour Contract 
Co - operative Society Ltd . (Contractor 
of Purupapani Limestone & Dolomite 
Quarry of SAIL ) in terminating the 


Tf the enquiry conducted against 
second party -workman with regard 
misconduct was fair and proper ? 


the 
to 


(NTTI - 


( ii }] 


# TRU TATUTA : 9647€ 25, 1995/4T17 O , 1916 


683 


2 . 


If the charges against the workman for 
which he had been dismissed froni ser 
vice have been establislied in the domes 
tic enquiry ? 
If the punishment of dismissal inflicted 
on the workman is highly disproportio 
nate to the misconduct allegedly proved 
against him ? 
To what relief, if any , the workman is 
crititled ? 


that evidence in this Award is not necessary be 
cause the deposition is a part and parcel of the 
record . Thus, the case is at a stage where the man 
ugenient has not proved the alleged misconducts 
covered by the charge sheet. Ext. 1. In the absence 
of proof and when there is no admission from the 
side of the workman it has to be held that the 
charges are not proved . Accordingly, the Isguc 
No . 2 is decided against the management. 


6 . At the instance of the management the 
abovenoted issue No. 1 was taken - up for consi 
deration as a preliminary issue. At that stage the 
management examined three witnesses and tender 
ed in evidence documents such as charge sheet 
(Ext . 1 ) , enquiry report dated 2 - 5 - 86 (Ext. 2 ) , 
second show -cause notice (Ext. 3 ) and the show 
cause furnished (Ext. 4 ) . At that stage the work 
man examined himself and also tendered in evi 
dence the copy of his removal order dated 26 - 7 - 86 
(Ext. A ) . The Issue No. 1 was decided and dis 
posed of vide order No. 40 dated 30 -6 - 94 in 
which this Tribunal recorded the finding that the 
principle of natural justice was violated and the 
workman was not afforded with proper and ade 
quate opportunity to defend in the domestic en 
quiry and as such , the domestic enquiry was not 
fair and proper. While recording such finding on 
Issue No. 1, the management was called upon to 
adduce evidence on merit of the charges. 


9 . Since the finding in Issuc No. 2 as aforesaid , 
is against the inanagement and since the miscon 
ducts have not been proved against the workman , 
therefore , the punishment of dismissal froin service 
infi .cted against the workman is not sustainable . 
Thus, while considering Issue No. 3 justifiability 
of the quantum of punishment is no more requir 
ed to be gone into save and except mentioning in 
the aforesaid manner that the workman is not 
liable to be punished because the management has 
failed to substantiate the charges . Under such cir 
cumstance, while considering Issue No. 4 regard 
ing the relief, it is found that since the allegations 
of misconduct are not proved and the order of 
dismissal is not sustainable , under such circum 
stance the workınan is entitled to reinstatement in 
service with back wages althroughout. 


10 . Accordingly, the Award is passed to the 
effect that the termination of service of the work 
inan (Kiron Shoy ) with effect from 24 - 9 - 85 is not 
justified and he is entitled to reinstatement in 
service with back wages for the entire period . 


Dictated & corrected by ine . 

P. K . TRIPATHY, Presiding Officer 


7. While hearing the dispute relating to the 
merit of the charges the management has deciined 
to adduce any further evidence. At that stage the 
workman further examined himself. Thus , the 
justifiability of the termination order from service 
of the workman is to be considered from the avail 
able evidenco in record .

changes relating to disobedience of orders, dis 
honesty etc ., the charges. Sipailarly , the other ex

ment do not prove the allegations against the 
workman . On the other hand , the workman in his 
further evidence dated 8 - 12 - 94 has categorically

In terms of Sec . 7 ( a ) of the I. D . Act, the appro

(4 ) Clause 9 (d ) clause 20 : 13 of the Bipartite 
settlement dated 19 - 10 - 66 reads that the temporary 
godown keepers and godown watchman , who are 
required to lookafter one or more godown . be 
longing generally to one party and whose salary 
and allowance are generally borne by the parties, 
who are owners of the goods, shall, if their work 
has been found satisfactory and if thcir services 
can be utilised to lookafter other godowns, in the 
same place or other places or in the clerical posts 
or any other sub -ordinnate cadre post as the case 
may be on completion of " ONE YEAR S SER 
VICE ” for giving preference for alsorption in the 
permanent service of the bank etc." 

(5 ) While such were the provisions of the bipar 
tite agreement, the management of Andhra Bank , 
rejected the genuine and legitimate claim of the 
workman in any post of Andhra Bank , vide the 
letter No . 666 20 A2 /3011 dnted 6 - 9 - 88 of Sri 
S V . S . Sarma, Personnel Manager, Andhra Bank , 
Central Office . Hyderabad stating that it is not possi 
ble to absorb the workman in their bank s service 
as all the appointments in sub -staff cadre , are being 
considered only if they are sponsored through em 
ployment exchange . In this connection the above 
stipulation is a general nature and does not appli 
cable in the present case of the workman as lie was 
already an employee of the bank and had put in 
three years service continuously . 


(9 ) The petitioner was appointed as Godown 
Watchman by the Regionai Manager is incorrect 
and denied . The bank provides terms loans and 
overdrafts to various companies to carry out their 
business and according to the terms and conditions 
of the loan agreements the stocks and goods kept in 
the godown are hypothecated to the bank . It is 
mandatory on the part of the borrower to cnsure 
the safety of the goods. Ms. Laxmi Oil Mils Pvt. 
Ltd ., Yanain hypothecated their goods to the res 
pondent Bank and that have appointed Mr. Hara 
Gopal the petitioner Herein , as watch and ward 
staff to lookafter the godown . The salary of the 
petitioner is paid to the debit of the borrower s ac 
count monthly at the request of the borrower him 
self. The services rendered by the petitioner cannot 
be termed as service rendered by him to the res 
pondent hank , it cannot be counted that he was in 
the Bank s service . He was engaged as sub -staff 
to lookafter the godowns of Mis. Laxmi Oil Mills 
(P ) Ltd .

का . प्रा . 568 -- प्रौद्योगिक विवाद अधिनियम , 
1947 ( 1947 का 14) की धारा 17 के अनुसरण में , 
केन्द्रीय सरकार राजपरा दरीबा माईन्स के प्रबन्धतंत्र के संबड 
नियोजमों और उनके कर्मकारों के बीच , अनुबंध में निर्दिष्ट 
प्रौद्योगिक विवाद में प्रौद्योगिक अधिकरण , उदयपुर के पंचपट 
को प्रमाणित करती है, जो केन्द्रीय सरकार को 2-- 2 - 95 
को प्राप्त हुआ था । 
[ संख्या एल - 29012/ 38/ 91- माई . भार . (विविध )] 

बी . एम . डेविड, डेस्क अधिकारी 


सजा के बिन्दु पर विद्धान अधिवक्ता प्रार्थी का कथन 
है कि 12 वर्ष की मेवा किये जाने के उपरान्त प्रार्थी को 
सेवामुक्त किया जाना न्यायोचित नहीं है ।विद्वान अभियानमा 
विपक्षी का तर्क है कि जो कर्मचारी अनुशासन में नहीं रहता 

और अपने अधिकारियों के साथ मारपीट कर दुर्व्यवहार करता 
है और जिसको समय - समय पर चेतावनी दी जा चुकी है , 
उसके पिरुब ऐमे पारोप प्रमाणित होने पर सेवामुक्ति का 
प्रावेश उपित एवं वैधानिक माना जाना चाहिये । । 


मैंने दोनों पक्ष के तर्क सने मरे द्वारा निकाले गये 
निष्कर्ष निम्न प्रकार है :--..

प्रारोपित प्रारोप की तुलना में एवं कर्मचारी द्वारा संस्थान 
में की गई पूर्ण समग्रकालीन सेवा की पृष्ट भूमि में एवं अन्य 
विवार की जा सकने वाली परिस्थितियों को ध्यान में रखकर 
मूल्यांकन किया जाना चाहिये । प्रस्तुत प्रकरण में प्रार्थी को जो

699 


वरिष्ट सहमियों के साथ गाली -गलोज की भाषा का प्रयोग 
करना व उच्छृखल व्यवहार करना तथा अपने वरिष्ठ 
अधिकारियों को धमकी देना अथवा माक्रमण करना सधा 
स्थाई आदेशों की अवहेलना करना है । 


बहत मुश्किल है । साथ ही संस्थान में ऐसे व्यक्तियों की पुन 
नियुक्ति भी बहुत कठिन हो जाती है । आरोपित प्रागेप में 
स्वयं प्रार्थी ने भी अपनी आई हुई चोटों का प्रमाणपत्र पेश 
किया है । यही नहीं घटना अवधि में वह मानसिक रूप में 
पीड़ित था और जनरल अस्पताल, उदयपुर में इलाज में भी 
रहा था । प्रार्थी ने स्टेटमेन्ट आफ क्लेम में अपने द्वारा 
दुर्व्यवहार के बारे में क्षमायामना भी की है । अतः 
सद्भाविक दृष्टिकोण को भी विस्तन नहीं किया जाना 
चाहिये । 


मैंने जांच अधिकारी द्वारा लिये गये साक्षीगण के बयानों 
एवं जिरह का गंभीरता से अवलोकन किया । केवलमान 
7- 7-90 एवं 23- 7- 90 की घटना के अलावा सामान्य रूप 
में प्रार्थी का आचरण ऐसा रहा हो जैसा कि आरोपों में 
बसागा है, प्रकट नहीं होगा । यही नहीं 12 वर्ष की सेवा पूर्ण 
करने पर इस दौरान जो भी प्रार्थी की अमंतोषप्रद मेवाएं 
सिद्ध करने के लिये प्रलेख प्रस्तुत किये है वे या तो प्रार्थी 
में मांगे गये स्पष्टीकरण अथवा उसके द्वारा दी गई सलाह से 
मयंधित है बावल मात्र एक चेतावनी का पन है । विवेतन का 
अभिप्राय यह है कि 12 वर्ष की सेवा अवधि में प्रार्थी को 
किसी प्रकार का माईनर दंड भी पारित नहीं किया गया है । 
हमके विपरीत प्रार्थी को उसकी मेवाओं के लिये प्रसंशा पत्र 
दिया गया है जो प्रदर्श - 3 है । मेया कार्य में सहकमियों अथवा 
उच्च अधिकारियों में कभी - कभार मनमुटाव होना या 
उत्तेजनापूर्ण या पावेशपर्ण क्षण प्राना स्वभाविक है । जहाँ तक 
7 - 5 - 90 और 23- 7 - 90 की घटना के प्रारोप सिख माने 
आने का संबंध है उसमें किसी स्तोत्र की प्राचायकता नहीं 
है परन्त . इसके लिये संवा समाप्ति के दंड से दंडित करना 
प्रार्थी को आर्थिक मृत्यु का आदेश पारित करने के समान है । 
क्योंकि इस अवस्था में अब प्रार्थी का अन्यत्र सेवारत होना 


उक्त विवेचन के आधार पर मैं प्रार्थी की सेवामुक्ति के 
आदेश को उचित नहीं पाता और प्रार्थी को दो वेतन वृद्धियां 
संचयी प्रभाव में रोकी जाने का संशोधित आदेश पारित करता 
हं । प्रार्थी को पुनः मेवा में लिये जाने का आदेश देता हं , 
परन्तु प्रार्थी के सेवा समाप्ति होने में सेवा में लिये जाने के 
दिन तक 50 प्रतिशत घेतन भुगतान किये जाने का प्रावण 
देता है । निलम्बन काल का बकाया भुगतान भी प्रार्थी प्राप्त 
नहीं कर सकेगा । 

परिस्थितियों के अनुसार दोनों पक्षकाराम खर्चा मुकदमा 
अपना - अपना वहन करेंगे । 

पंचाट सचनार्थ केन्द्र सरकार को प्रेषित किया जाये । 

पंचाट प्रान दिनांक 14 - 12- 1994 को मुले न्यायालय में 
लिखा गया जाकर सुनाया गया ।
